<MANDATORY_CONSTRAINTS>
1. ZERO-GC HOT PATHS: The translation of a 64-byte envelope into a game-state mutation or `SignalBus` payload must generate 0 bytes of managed allocation. 5. NO BINARY SWITCHES: The rate at which mod commands are processed or dropped must scale continuously based on `HomeostasisBrain.GlobalQualityWeight`. Do not use `if (lowEndHardware)`. Task 11: CONTINUOUS_SCALABILITY_TEXT_SPEED. The speed of the "Dear Lie" typewriter effect (Task 08) must be governed by `HomeostasisBrain.GlobalQualityWeight`. If the system has headroom, it renders chunks of text instantly.
Task 12: DYNAMIC_TOKEN_REPLACEMENT. Lore text may contain tokens (e.g., `^PLAYER_NAME^`). Your UTF-8 decoding loop (Task 07) must detect the `^` character. When found, it pauses decoding, looks up the requested token hash in a fast NativeHashMap, inserts the corresponding characters into the `Span<char>`, and resumes decoding. This provides dynamic text formatting without using `string.Replace`.

Format this float directly into the `Span<char>` using `TryFormat`.

It must be perfectly aligned so the networking agent (Agent 108) can snapshot the `EncyclopediaStateDTO` buffer using a blind `UnsafeUtility.MemCpy`. If a network rollback occurs (e.g., the player actually didn't pick up the datapad due to lag), the bitmask is rewound, and the lore locks again instantly.
